Rules (Jake): primary sources first; the four-step rule before anything is called untraceable; one item at a time; a wrong stored number is fixed or removed before any new number is added. Figures below were read 2026-10-27 unless dated; "fixture" = the P1 build store (Jake's 10-13 dumps).

### D6 — Round D6 (Jake, 2026-10-27, head 08de221), old → new

| Item | Area | Old | New |
|---|---|---|---|
| D6-1 | Covered days (SPEC §1 rule 9) | build_workbook._window_coverage, SHARED by every non-monthly flow's covered_days / q0..q4_covered_days (and so _annualise, the coverage caveat and the same-span guard): the days from the series' FIRST ROW to the window end — an interior day with no row counted as covered (Ethereum's Q0 consensus rewards: 4 missing days annualised as present). Only config.DENSE_DAILY_FLOWS counted days held. | The SAME function, for every daily-cadence flow: the span as before, LESS every interior day no row covers. A row covers its own day, or its declared span (`[span=Nd]`: N days ending on its date); a DERIVED row (a view's output) covers what its inputs' rows on the same date cover (wiring.view_edges — e.g. Hyperliquid's buyback $ = tokens × price inherits the tokens' `[span=4d]` on 10-05), never a day the derived series itself lacks. A day with no row is a HOLE, never a zero. Unchanged on purpose: (1) days AFTER the last row (the lag allowance — staleness reports a stopped series); (2) a scan read from genesis (config.scanned_from_genesis: every scanned day is read, a quiet day is a measured zero); (3) weekly / epoch / monthly series (period-dated rows: the period is the coverage); (4) DENSE_DAILY_FLOWS (already counted). A declared source hole (config.declared_source_hole) is subtracted only for its days that HOLD a row, so no day is subtracted twice. Holes are named on the row ("Q0 COVERS n OF 90 DAYS — holes: <days>"). |
| D6-2 | Morpho "+54% on 2026-10-22" | D5-3a: not identified. | The Review Queue change_threshold item on Morpho **volume_usd** — CoinGecko's daily TRADING VOLUME of the MORPHO token (market_chart total_volumes; the point dated D 00:00 UTC is the 24h of trading on D−1). Primary data, each exchange's own daily candles for 10-21 vs 10-14 (UTC): Coinbase MORPHO-USD $1,245,382 vs $761,238 (+64%); OKX MORPHO-USDT 1,152,588 vs 486,294 USDT (+137%); Upbit KRW-MORPHO ₩755.2M vs ₩320.7M (+136%) — a real, market-wide rise in trading on 10-21 (Binance and Bybit refuse this location: not read). Not a read fault; volume_usd is market activity, out of the level-break check by design. Decision for the queue: ACCEPT, with that evidence (config REVIEW_ANSWERS). |

### P2a — the Charlie build (Jake, 2026-10-27, head ba10503), old → new

The reader (token_metrics.xlsx) for a public audience. Every P1 rule kept: numbers only in value cells, else a code (n/a · blocked · not wired · by design · parked; "unranked" in a rank column) with the reason in the comment; headers ≤ 35 characters; $m/$bn, %, 0.0x; confidence as fill; every number's comment names its audit cell; ranks within the primary archetype by its key. The audit file's tabs and columns are unchanged.

| Item | Area | Old | New |
|---|---|---|---|
| P2a-A1 | Rank with no measured key | Ranked 2–7 beside an n/a key. | Reads "unranked" (the reason in the comment) and sorts last. |
| P2a-A2 | Empty audit cells | A blank reader cell (payout for Ethereum, Plume, Morpho, Aethir; World Mobile's dilution). | A code with its reason: "by design" where the column does not apply (no revenue-funded holder value → no payout), "n/a" where it is not formed; a parked project's "n/a" names the parking. BLANK-UNEXPLAINED on the reader is counted every build (READER_STATS blank_unexplained) and is 0. |
| P2a-A3 | Payout above 1.0x | No comment. | The comment says what funds the excess, from the gross payout on fees: gross ≤ 1 → paid out of fees the revenue basis does not count; gross > 1 → treasury or revenue from before the window. |

| P2a-A5 | Pre-minted net supply change 0 | No comment. | "A pre-minted supply: nothing is minted, so the net supply change is 0; releases appear under dilution, not issuance" (emissions_model distributed_from_premint, or a fixed-supply cell). |
